## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

This is a well-built facilitation deck: a consistent Watch, Try, Share pattern, time badges, a single running florist example, and a clear before/after prompt ladder (slide 17) make it easy to follow live. The build recipe is repeated as a five-step tracker (slides 44 to 49), so the route is coherent and each activity page tells people what to do. Weaknesses are many near-empty pages with small text and large dead space (slides 8, 19, 26, 28, 30, 38), several tiny and unreadable screenshots (slides 14, 15, 28, 36), and about a dozen gradient divider and name pages that add length without content.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the Task, Context, Expectation colour cards (slide 16), the Okay, Better, Best highlighted prompt ladder (slide 17), the numbered build recipe table (slide 44) and the Watch, Try, Share structure with time badges.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge the body text and screenshots on the demo pages (slides 14, 15, 28, 29, 31) and fill or tighten the large empty areas on the text-light slides such as 8, 19 and 26, where the small text floats far from its title.

**What would change the score:** I would cut or merge the repeated gradient divider and speaker pages (slides 2 to 4, 52, 53, 62, 65) and trim the duplicate Watch, Try, Share route slides (23, 42, 50).

_Confidence medium. 69 of 69 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a welcome to the SME AI Accelerator in London on April 29, 2026, introducing three speakers from OpenAI and Enterprise Nation and Booking.com, followed by a short room-introduction exercise and a stated goal of leaving with one reusable solution. It then moves through an agenda covering ChatGPT foundations, responsible use and hallucination risks, and prompting, with a worked example of rewriting weak prompts into task, context and expectation. Next comes a demo of two workflows for a fictional London florist, customer feedback analysis and a spring promotion campaign, each with input, output and hands-on activities. After a coffee break, the session turns to building a custom GPT or project through a build recipe covering job definition, context, output format, guardrails and testing, followed by a showcase of local small business owners, individual build time, sharing and a closing call to continue learning. The slides rely heavily on step-by-step callouts, paired exercises and time-boxed activities, so the structure is easy to follow as a training run-through.

## Questions about this deck

**What does the OpenAI SME AI Accelerator deck say about the Harbour Bloom Florist example?**

Slide 24 describes Harbour Bloom Florist as an independent neighborhood florist in London that creates bouquets, gift arrangements, and event flowers. It serves local customers and nearby businesses and wants to boost sales and handle marketing faster.

**How many slides does the OpenAI SME AI Accelerator deck have?**

The deck has 69 pages. The agenda on slide 5 lists eight sections, from Welcome through Showcase and wrap.

**What does the OpenAI SME AI Accelerator deck say about responsible use of AI?**

Slide 12 says to keep a human in the loop for important work, obtain consent before sharing someone else's voice or data, and watch for bias and perspective. It also advises knowing your data controls, protecting sensitive personal data, seeking expert review for legal, medical, or financial advice, and asking for sources when you need current answers.

**What is the OpenAI SME AI Accelerator deck's build recipe for creating a custom GPT?**

Slide 44 lists a five-step build recipe: define the job, add context and knowledge, specify the output, add guardrails, and test for reliability. Slide 45 expands step 1 with questions about who the tool is for, when they will use it, and what success looks like.

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a white slide. A black pill tag sits at the top left, with the headline stacked beneath it, left-aligned to a margin of about 2.7% of the width. The blue prompt list sits in the lower right half, starting at about 51% of the width, with a check-icon bullet to the left of each item. The footer runs along the bottom with the OpenAI wordmark and 'SME AI Accelerator' at the left and the page number at the right. The lower-left quadrant is empty.

**Visuals**

Two outlined check-in-circle icons, black stroke, one beside each blue prompt item. Icon 1 is at about x 47.5, y 51.1, w 2.3, h 3.8. Icon 2 is at about x 47.5, y 68.3, w 2.3, h 3.8. Each icon is a thin black circle outline containing a black check mark. The OpenAI wordmark in the footer is a small black logotype. No photographs, charts or other graphics.

**Design**

Background is pure white #FFFFFF with no gradient or texture. The '5 MINUTES' tag is a solid black #000000 pill with fully rounded ends (corner radius about half its height) and white #FFFFFF monospace capitals. The headline is black #000000 in a geometric grotesque sans-serif (close to Suisse Int'l or Helvetica Neue), regular weight, about 8.9% of slide height per line, with line pitch about 9.6%. The blue prompt text is #3D8DFF in the same sans-serif, regular weight, about 5.4% of slide height, with about 1.1 lines of space between items. Footer text is black #000000, about 1.5% of slide height. No shadows or gradients. Accent colour is #3D8DFF.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF on left, light blue #B7D4FE panel on right. Title text black #000000, body text black #000000, accent blue #3D8DFF (button) and #B7D4FE (panel). Typeface: geometric grotesque sans-serif similar to Inter or Aeonik, regular weight for title and bullets, medium for the footer. Title size about 5.4% of slide height (cap height about 3.8%), body bullets about 3.5% of slide height, sub-bullets about 3.2%, footer about 1.5%. ### Slide 10

**Text**

Let’s dive in
OpenAI
SME AI Accelerator
11

**Layout**

Single centred title on a full-bleed image, set in the vertical and horizontal middle of the slide. A footer row sits along the bottom edge: the OpenAI wordmark at left, then the footer text 'SME AI Accelerator' beside it, with the page number at far right. Margins are about 2.7% at left and 3% at right, and the footer baseline is near 95% of slide height.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed abstract gradient image covering the whole slide, with no recognisable subject. It shows soft, blurred diagonal ribbons of light running from upper left to lower right: a saturated hot pink band at the top left, a violet-pink band across the top centre, a blue glow at the top centre-right and a deeper blue glow at lower centre-right, with pale lavender and light sky-blue areas between them. The lighting is soft and diffuse, the framing is full-bleed and uncropped by any box, and there is no overlay beyond the white text. A pink-to-blue diagonal sheen runs through the lower left corner. The title is rendered as an image of white text (1012 by 144 pixels). Dominant colours are lavender #B9A4FF, periwinkle blue #7FB2FF, hot pink #FF6FB0, cornflower blue #4D8DFF and pale pink #F2C4F0.

**Design**

Background is a blurred multi-colour gradient photograph with no border or frame. The title is pure white #FFFFFF in a regular-weight geometric sans-serif (close to a DM Sans or Sora style), with normal case and no letter-spacing change, about 19% of slide height in size. The footer is white #FFFFFF: 'OpenAI' in a bold sans-serif at small size, and 'SME AI Accelerator' and the page number in a regular-weight sans-serif at about 1.5% of slide height. There are no line or shape styles, no corner radii, no shadows and no visible gradient on the text itself.

### Slide 11

**Text**

ChatGPT can help you achieve superhero efficiency
It's a Superhero Suit
It's Having 1000 Assistants
It’s Got Your Back…& Future
OpenAI
SME AI Accelerator

**Layout**

Three equal columns of square images sit below a single-line title at the top left, with each column headed by a caption line. The title starts at x 2.7% and y 4.3%. The captions sit at y 29.9%, and the images run from about y 37% to 90%. Margins are about 2.7% left and 2.6% right. The footer is at bottom left: the OpenAI wordmark, then the text 'SME AI Accelerator' beside it. There is no slide number visible. The middle of the slide between the title and captions is empty white.

**Visuals**

Three square illustrations, each with rounded corners of about 1% of slide width and no border or shadow. Left: a muscular figure seen from behind and partly cropped at the top, in a flowing red cape, against a bright white and pale pink glow with soft light rays; the cape sweeps from the upper left down to the lower right. Middle: a dense crowd of silhouetted people seen from behind, filling the frame in blue, violet and magenta haze. A single figure in the centre glows white and is lit from behind; a woman with long hair stands to its right; the crowd fades into the light at the top. Right: two climbers on a rocky peak in a misty lavender sky; one climber in a backpack reaches down to haul up a second climber, who reaches up with an outstretched hand. The rocks are dark purple-grey in the foreground. No charts, tables, logos or icons other than the OpenAI wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Title is a regular-weight geometric grotesque sans-serif (close to Söhne or Inter), black #000000, about 5.4% of slide height, sentence case. Column captions are the same family at about 3.5% of slide height, black #000000, regular weight with key phrases in bold ('Superhero Suit', '1000 Assistants', 'Got Your Back'). Footer wordmark 'OpenAI' is bold sans-serif at about 1.5% height, black; 'SME AI Accelerator' is regular at about 1.5% height, black. Accent colours come only from the illustrations: red #D8343F, violet #6B3FD0, lavender #C9B8F0, white #FFFFFF. Shape styles: image frames have rounded corners and no stroke.

### Slide 12

**Text**

Responsible use of AI / Keep a human in the loop for / important work / Obtain consent before sharing / someone else’s voice or data / Watch for bias and perspective / Know your data controls, and protect / sensitive personal data / Seek expert review for legal, medical, / or financial advice / Ask for sources when you need current / answers / OpenAI / Confidential and proprietary. Internal use only.

**Layout**

Single-column title block at top left with the title starting at about 2.7% from the left edge and 4.3% from the top. Below a large empty band, six white cards sit in a two-column by three-row grid: left column x 2.7% to 48.7%, right column x 51.4% to 97.4%, with a gutter of about 2.7%. Rows are at y 34.2%, 53.9% and 73.6%, each card 16.4% tall with about 3.3% vertical gap. Each card holds an icon at its left and left-aligned two-line or one-line text. The footer sits at the bottom with the OpenAI wordmark at the left margin and the confidentiality line beside it, with no slide number visible.

**Visuals**

Six line icons, all drawn in black outline strokes of roughly uniform medium weight, one per card. Top-left card: a simple standing person outline (head circle over a rounded shoulder arc), about 3% wide and 5% tall at x about 5.5% y about 39.5%. Top-right card: a notepad or clipboard outline with a spiral binding on its left edge and a checkmark inside a rounded square, about 3.5% wide at x about 53.8% y about 39.5%. Middle-left card: an eye outline with a circular pupil, about 3.5% wide at x about 5.4% y about 59.5%. Middle-right card: a hexagonal badge outline containing a person silhouette, about 3% wide at x about 53.8% y about 58.5%. Bottom-left card: a graduation mortarboard cap outline with a tassel on the right, about 3.5% wide at x about 5.4% y about 78.5%. Bottom-right card: a magnifying glass outline with the handle at lower right, about 3.5% wide at x about 54.5% y about 78.5%. Each card also has a soft drop shadow, rendered from a 1000 by 257 pixel shadow image spanning the card bottom edge, which is not a visible picture.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or pattern. Title in a geometric grotesque sans-serif, regular weight, black #000000, cap height about 5.4% of slide height at size about 5.37% of slide height. Card text in the same family, regular weight, black #000000, size about 3.52% of slide height, line spacing about 4.2% of slide height between baselines. Cards are white #FFFFFF rectangles with rounded corners of about 0.8% of slide width, no border, and a faint diffuse grey shadow beneath (about #E6E6E6 to #D0D0D0 at the lower edge). Icons are black #000000 outlines with rounded line caps. Footer wordmark OpenAI in bold grotesque sans, black; footer line in regular sans at size about 1.48% of slide height, black. Accent colour: none beyond black.

What is prompting? / Prompting is the process of giving instructions, context, or inputs to guide a language model's responses. / In other words, it’s providing all the information of what you have and what you need to a blank canvas. / OpenAI / SME AI Accelerator / 16 / [Chat card, right, text in image: What is prompting? / Prompting is the practice of giving instructions or inputs to a large language model (LLM) like ChatGPT to guide its response. A prompt can be as simple as a question or as complex as a multi-step scenario with constraints, goals, and examples. The way you structure a prompt has a significant impact on the quality, relevance, and accuracy of the output. / 🔍 What Is Prompting? / At its core, prompting is how you communicate with an LLM to get the result you want. It's similar to writing a query or giving directions: / Simple prompt: “Summarize this article.” / Detailed prompt: “Act as a legal analyst. Summarize the key privacy concerns in the following article in bullet points, with citations.” / Prompts can be phrased in natural language and don’t require programming knowledge, which makes them powerful tools for non-technical users.]

### Slide 16

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A three-part Task, Context and Expectation layout makes the anatomy of a good prompt easy to scan at a glance.
- Worth keeping: Keep the Task, Context, Expectation colour cards (slide 16), the Okay, Better, Best highlighted prompt ladder (slide 17), the numbered build recipe table (slide 44) and the Watch, Try, Share structure with time badges.

**Text**

Anatomy of a great prompt
Task
What should
ChatGPT do?
Suggest 5 low-cost ways to
increase repeat customers
this month.
Context
What should
ChatGPT know?
We’re a small business
in London.
Customers:
Channels: email, social,
in-store, referrals
Budget:
Expectation
What should the
output look like?
Output: a table with idea,
channel, and one
sample message.
OpenAI
SME AI Accelerator
17

**Layout**

Single title at top left, left-aligned at about 2.7% from the left edge and 4.3% from the top, followed by three equal-width columns (each about 29.8% wide, 2.7% / 35.1% / 67.6% x-start) with equal gaps of about 2.6% and a common top at 16.1% and bottom at 90.4%. Each column holds a bold title word, a two-line regular question and a body text block starting at about 45.8%. The footer sits at about 94.5% with the OpenAI wordmark and 'SME AI Accelerator' at left and the page number 17 at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

none (three flat rounded-corner panels serve as text containers only; no photographs, charts, icons or diagrams)

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Titles and headings black #000000 in a regular-weight grotesque sans-serif (closest common: Helvetica-like grotesque), the word 'Task', 'Context' and 'Expectation' in bold, questions in regular weight; title size about 5.4% of slide height (capitals about 0.7 of that); body text about 3.15% of slide height, regular, black #000000, left-aligned with line spacing about 1.2. Three flat panels: left panel #D0EDFA (pale blue), middle panel #FDD8B9 (pale peach), right panel #CEF4C6 (pale green), each with rounded corners of roughly 2% of slide width radius, no border, no shadow, no gradient. Footer text about 1.5% of slide height, black #000000, sans-serif.

### Slide 17

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Shows the same request in three escalating versions side by side, so the effect of added context is visible without extra explanation.
- Review: This is a well-built facilitation deck: a consistent Watch, Try, Share pattern, time badges, a single running florist example, and a clear before/after prompt ladder (slide 17) make it easy to follow live.
- Worth keeping: Keep the Task, Context, Expectation colour cards (slide 16), the Okay, Better, Best highlighted prompt ladder (slide 17), the numbered build recipe table (slide 44) and the Watch, Try, Share structure with time badges.

**Text**

Okay
Task
Help me hire a barista.
Better
Task + context
Help me hire a barista. I run a café with 8 employees. We need a part-time
barista who can handle morning rush and be friendly with customers.
Best
Task + context +
expectation
Help me hire a barista. I run a café with 8 employees. We need a part-time
barista who can handle morning rush and be friendly with customers. Write
(1) a job post, (2) a 20 minute interview script with a simple scorecard, and
(3) a one-week onboarding plan. Keep it realistic for a small shop.
OpenAI
SME AI Accelerator

**Layout**

Three horizontal rows, evenly spaced, each pairing a left-column label (x about 2.7%) with a wide white input card (x 27.0% to 97.4%, about 70% of slide width). Row tops are at about 4.8%, 35.1% and 65.3% of slide height, each card about 25% high. Left margin is about 2.7% and right margin about 2.6%. There is no title bar; the labels Okay, Better and Best are the row headings. Footer sits bottom-left at about 94.4% to 96% height: OpenAI wordmark, then the text SME AI Accelerator. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Three identical rounded white input cards stacked vertically, each with a soft blue-grey drop shadow beneath, and each containing one prompt. Each card has a black circular send button with a white upward arrow at its bottom-right corner (about 2.8% by 5% of the slide). Card 1 contains the single line 'Help me hire a barista.' with no highlight. Card 2 contains the prompt with the added context sentence 'I run a café with 8 employees. We need a part-time barista who can handle morning rush and be friendly with customers.' highlighted in pale yellow #FEEFC9 bands behind the text. Card 3 contains the full prompt; its added text 'Write (1) a job post, (2) a 20 minute interview script with a simple scorecard, and (3) a one-week onboarding plan. Keep it realistic for a small shop.' is highlighted in the same pale yellow, the highlight starting on the word Write at the end of line 2. The highlight bands are flat rectangles with square edges behind each text line. The OpenAI logo is a small black bold wordmark at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid light blue #B7D4FE covering the whole slide. Flat, minimal, light-blue background with white rounded cards and black sans-serif text. Background #B7D4FE. Cards #FFFFFF with corner radius about 1% of slide width and a soft diffuse shadow. Highlights #FEEFC9. Labels bold black #000000 (Okay, Better, Best) at about 3.5% of slide height, cap height about 2.5%. Sub-labels regular black #000000 at the same size. Prompt body text regular black #000000 at about 3.15% of slide height, line spacing about 3.8% of slide height. Typeface is a modern geometric-grotesque sans-serif similar to OpenAI Sans or Söhne. Footer text regular at about 1.48% of slide height. No gradients, no strokes, no icons other than the send arrows.

**Layout**

Two-column split with a white left panel (about 40.6% of width) and a full-height saturated blue right panel (#3D8DFF) starting at x 40.6%. The left column holds a black pill label at top-left, a large title and subtitle stacked at upper-left, and a footer row at bottom-left; the left column below the subtitle is empty. The right panel holds four equal white cards stacked vertically with even gaps (about 2.2% of height), left edge at x 43.2% and right edge at x 97.3%, each card spanning about 18.5% of height, with the first card top at y 4.8%. Left margin is about 2.7%, top margin about 4.8%. Footer logo and text sit at bottom-left at y about 94.5%, and the page number sits bottom-right at x 96.6%, y 94.4%, on the blue panel.

**Visuals**

Four white rectangular prompt cards, each with a black circular arrow button at its lower-right corner. Each button is a solid black circle (about 3.9% of width) containing a white upward-pointing arrow (thin stroke, straight shaft with chevron head, pointing up). The buttons sit at x 92.7%, at y 15.1%, 37.4%, 59.2% and 81.5%, overlapping the lower-right corner of each card. Soft blue-tinted drop shadows sit beneath each card, sourced from four 1000 by 242 pixel shadow images at x 41.5% stacked at y 2.0%, 24.2%, 46.4% and 68.7%. The black pill label at top-left is a solid black rounded capsule with white monospace capitals. No photographs, charts, tables or diagrams.

**Design**

Background: Split background: white #FFFFFF on the left 40.6% and blue #3D8DFF on the right 59.4%. Two-tone layout: white left field (#FFFFFF) and saturated blue right field (#3D8DFF). Primary text and title colour is black (#000000); body and prompt text is black (#000000); the pill label has white text (#FFFFFF) on black (#000000). The only accent colour is the blue panel #3D8DFF. Typefaces: the title and subtitle and prompts use a geometric grotesque sans-serif in regular weight, close to Suisse Int'l or Helvetica Neue; the pill label uses a monospace sans in regular weight, close to IBM Plex Mono or Space Mono, in capitals. Title is about 8.9% of slide height; subtitle and prompt text about 5.4% and 4.3% of slide height; footer and page number about 1.5%. Cards are white with rounded corners (radius about 1% of slide height), no outline, and a soft blue drop shadow. The pill has fully rounded ends.

What improved
the result?
What did you change, and
how did it help?
Read your ‘before’ and
‘after’ prompts.
OpenAI
SME AI Accelerator

**Layout**

Single white slide with a left-aligned headline in the upper left and a right-aligned blue checklist in the lower right; the lower-left area is empty. A black pill label sits at the top-left margin of about 2.7% of width, and a footer row at about 94.5% of height holds the OpenAI wordmark and running title on the left and the page number at the far right.

**Visuals**

Two identical check-in-circle icons, one beside each blue bullet line. Each is a black outlined circle about 2.3% of slide width and 4% of slide height, with a black check mark inside, stroke about 0.2% of slide height, no fill. Icon 1 sits at about x 55.8, y 51.1. Icon 2 sits at about x 55.8, y 68.3. No photographs, charts or other graphics.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF covering the whole slide. Plain white background with no gradient, texture or shadows. Headline is black #000000 in a geometric grotesque sans-serif (Suisse-like), regular weight, lowercase-led sentence case, large, with a tight two-line setting. Body checklist is blue #3D8DFF in the same sans-serif, regular weight, at about 5.4% of slide height with a line gap of about 1.1 times the size. The pill label is white #FFFFFF text in a monospace face (Space Mono-like), all capitals, on a black #000000 pill with fully rounded ends. Footer text is black #000000 at about 1.5% of slide height in the same sans-serif. Accent colour is blue #3D8DFF.

### Slide 20

**Text**

Takeaway
Great prompts reveal
hidden context.
Pull out the business context still
stuck in your head.
Ask yourself:
● What do I know that
ChatGPT doesn’t?
● What would help it
understand my business?
● What would a good answer
actually look like?
OpenAI
SME AI Accelerator
21

**Layout**

Two-column split: a white left area holds the title at top left and a large headline in the middle-left, while a light blue right panel occupies the rightmost 35% of the slide and holds the body copy and bulleted questions. The 'Takeaway' title sits at top left with about 2.7% left margin, all left-aligned text starts at the same left edge, and a footer row at the bottom holds the OpenAI logo, 'SME AI Accelerator' and the page number 21 at bottom right.

**Visuals**

No charts, photographs or diagrams. One flat light blue rectangle (#B7D4FE at 50% opacity, rendering as about #DBEAFE on white) fills the right 35% of the slide full height and acts as a column background. Bottom left is the OpenAI wordmark logo in bold black, set small. Three black filled circle bullets (●) mark the questions in the right panel.

**Design**

Background: Split background: solid white #FFFFFF across the slide with a light blue #DBEAFE (from #B7D4FE at 50% opacity) panel covering the right 35%. Clean minimal layout on a white background. Titles and body text are black #000000, with the only accent being the pale blue #DBEAFE-equivalent panel on the right. Typeface is a geometric grotesque sans-serif (close to a Helvetica/Inter-style sans) in regular weight, with bold used only for 'Ask yourself:'. The headline is about 8.9% of slide height, the 'Takeaway' title about 5.4%, body text about 3.5%, and footer text about 1.5%. No corner radii, shadows, gradients or visible lines.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide. The title sits top-left at about 4.3% from the top and 2.7% from the left margin, spanning two lines. The left column holds the florist name, a description and a three-item bullet list, left-aligned from the same 2.7% margin. The right column holds a rounded-corner illustration box at 51.4% to 97.4% horizontally and 22.8% to 77.3% vertically. The footer runs along the bottom at about 94.5%, with the OpenAI wordmark and 'SME AI Accelerator' on the left and the page number '25' at the right margin.

**Visuals**

One flat, soft-style illustration (768 by 512 px) on the right. Subject: a smiling woman florist with a shoulder-length brown bob, eyes lowered, wearing a navy apron over a white t-shirt. She holds and arranges a bouquet wrapped in kraft paper with peach roses, white roses and green foliage. Behind her at left, a grey hanging sign reads 'Open'. Left of the bouquet are grey galvanised buckets holding pink and peach tulips, white flowers and peach roses. On the right wall are two shelves: a small potted plant and bottles on the top shelf, and two kraft-wrapped bouquets tied with white ribbon on the lower shelf. A clipboard hangs on the wall. The counter is light wood with a navy card terminal, a kraft box, a spool of twine, a notebook and a pencil cup. Lighting is soft and even, from the front. Dominant colours: cream #FBF7F2, navy #1F2F4A, kraft tan #C8A77A, peach #F2B8A0, sage green #8FB08A. The image is not cropped; it sits in a box with rounded corners of about 1.5% of slide width and a faint cream tint. No overlay.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Titles and body text are black #000000. Accents: the navy #1F2F4A and kraft tones appear only in the illustration. The typeface is a geometric grotesque sans-serif (close to OpenAI Sans), regular weight throughout. Title text is about 5.4% of slide height, body text about 3.5%, footer and page number about 1.5%. Shapes: the photo box has rounded corners and no border or shadow. No gradients.

**Text**

you can reuse

campaign

26

**Layout**

Two equal rounded cards sit side by side in the right two-thirds of the slide, starting at about 19% and ending at about 97.4% of the width, spanning y 29.8% to 90.5%. The left column from 0% to about 19% is empty. The title sits top-left at x 2.7%, y 4.3%, set on two lines with a left margin of about 2.7%. Each card holds a pill label at top, a two-line heading, a two-line body sentence and a three-icon flow row along the bottom, all left-aligned to the card's inner margin of about 2.6%. The footer runs along the bottom: OpenAI wordmark at left (x 2.7%), then SME AI Accelerator at x 8.2%, and the page number 26 right-aligned at about x 96.4% to 97.4%, all on a baseline near y 94.5%.

**Visuals**

Two rounded rectangular cards with flat fills, not photographs. Card 1 (left): filled pink, carrying the label Workflow 1, heading Customer feedback to action plan, and body text. Card 2 (right): filled mint, carrying the label Workflow 2, heading Spring flower sale campaign, and body text. Each card has a three-step icon flow: a circular light-tint badge containing an eye icon (line-drawn eye with a central pupil ring), then a straight black arrow pointing right, then a circular badge with a sparkle-cursor icon (cursor arrow with radiating short strokes, indicating a click), then a straight black arrow pointing right, then a circular badge with a group-of-three-people icon (three outline figures with heads and shoulders). Badges are about 6.8% of slide height in diameter. Card 1 badges: eye at x 21.5%, y 79.0%; cursor at x 36.0%, y 79.0%; people at x 50.6%, y 79.0%. Card 1 arrows run from about x 25.4% to 35.4% and from about x 39.9% to 49.9% at y 82.4%. Card 2 badges: eye at x 61.9%, cursor at x 76.5%, people at x 91.0%, all at y 79.0%. Card 2 arrows run from about x 65.8% to 75.8% and from about x 80.3% to 90.4% at y 82.4%. Icons and arrows are black line art. The measured file also contains two light blue #B8D4FE fills at 50% opacity at x 18.9% and x 59.4% (y 29.8%, 37.9 by 60.6%) that sit directly beneath the cards; they are not distinctly visible in the render.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient, texture or imagery. Card 1 fill pink #F5BCE6; card 2 fill mint #B7F5E9. Pill label on card 1 is magenta #C31A8B with white text; pill on card 2 is dark teal-green #0A856B with white text. Title and headings are black #000000; body text is black #000000; footer text black #000000. Icon badges are a lighter tint of each card colour (about #FCE4F4 on pink, #E0FAF4 on mint). Arrows and icon strokes are black, about 1 px at slide scale. Typeface is a geometric grotesque sans-serif in regular weight (close to Söhne or Graphik style), with sentence case and no letter-spacing changes. Title and card headings are about 5.4% of slide height (cap height roughly 3.8%); body text is about 3.5% of slide height; pill labels are about 2.4% (semibold white); footer text about 1.5%. Cards have large corner radii of about 1.5 to 2% of slide height, with no visible shadow. Pills are fully rounded capsules.

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are many near-empty pages with small text and large dead space (slides 8, 19, 26, 28, 30, 38), several tiny and unreadable screenshots (slides 14, 15, 28, 36), and about a dozen gradient divider and name pages that add length without content.

**Text**

Workflow 1
Watch
Customer feedback
to action plan
Goal: Turn reviews, messages, and a simple sales
snapshot into clear actions and draft replies.
Input
Numbers: sales data, feedback ratings
Words: Google reviews, customer
comments, policies
Output
Top themes (with examples)
Top 3 operational fixes
Draft replies for negative reviews
A weekly staff checklist
OpenAI
SME AI Accelerator

**Layout**

Two-column split slide. The left white column carries the pill tag and section title at top left, with the goal statement lower down, and the right column is a full-height pink panel starting at 48.7% of slide width holding the Input and Output rows. Left margin is about 2.7% of width. Footer runs along the bottom at about 94.5% height with the OpenAI wordmark and 'SME AI Accelerator' at left and the page number at bottom right inside the pink panel.

**Visuals**

Icon: a black outline eye (almond-shaped outline with a small circular pupil, stroke about 2 px) centred inside a light pink circle, sitting to the right of the pill tag at the top. Logo: the OpenAI wordmark in bold black at bottom left. No photographs, charts or diagrams. Shapes: a fully rounded magenta pill behind 'Workflow 1' and a full-height pink rectangle forming the right panel.

**Design**

Background white on the left half with a flat pink #F5BCE6 panel on the right half. Titles and body text are black #000000; accent is magenta #C31A8B on the pill and light pink #F5BCE6 on the icon circle. Typeface is a geometric grotesque sans-serif in regular weight, with bold used for the 'Goal', 'Input' and 'Output' labels and the pill text. Title size is about 5.4% of slide height, body text about 3.2%, footer about 1.5%. Shapes have no outlines, shadows or gradients; pill corners are fully rounded.

**Design**

Background: Left white #FFFFFF; right pastel gradient from orange #F4A07A through pink #FFB3E6 to lavender #C9B8FF. White background (#FFFFFF) on the left, soft pastel gradient panel on the right. Title and body text are black #000000; the caption and grey prompt line are #8C8C8C; the accent is magenta #C31A8B on the pill and pale pink #F5B8E3 on the icon disc. Typeface is a geometric grotesque sans-serif similar to Söhne or Helvetica, regular weight for title, subtitle and body; bold for 'Workflow 1' and 'Add context'. Title height about 5.4% of slide height (cap height about 3.8%); body text about 3.2%; footer about 1.5%. Shapes have fully rounded ends on the pill and circles, card corner radius about 1% of slide height, a soft shadow under the card, and no gradients on shapes other than the backdrop.

### Slide 30

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are many near-empty pages with small text and large dead space (slides 8, 19, 26, 28, 30, 38), several tiny and unreadable screenshots (slides 14, 15, 28, 36), and about a dozen gradient divider and name pages that add length without content.

**Text**

Workflow 1
Watch
Ask for a summary
Next, ask ChatGPT for a simple summary.
Example asks
● What stands out in this data?
● What patterns do you see?
● What should this business pay / attention to?
● What other context would improve / your analysis?
OpenAI
SME AI Accelerator
31

**Layout**

Two-column split at 48.7% of slide width: a white left column with a header row, title, and subtitle, and a full-height pink right panel holding a labelled bullet list in its lower-middle area. The header row (pill button, eye icon, 'Watch') sits at the top left with the title below it, left-aligned at x 2.7%. The footer runs along the bottom: the OpenAI wordmark and 'SME AI Accelerator' at left, and the page number '31' at bottom right inside the pink panel. Left margin is about 2.7%.

**Visuals**

Four non-text elements. (1) A solid magenta pill-shaped button #C31A8B at top left, fully rounded ends, with the white bold label 'Workflow 1' centred on it. (2) A pale pink circle #F5BCE6 next to 'Watch', holding a black outline eye icon: an almond-shaped eye outline with a circular iris ring and a central pupil, stroke black, about 0.3% of slide height thick. (3) A full-height flat pink panel #F5BCE6 covering the right 51.3% of the slide, with no border, gradient or shadow. (4) The OpenAI wordmark in bold black at bottom left. There are no photographs, charts, or diagrams.

**Design**

Background: Split background: white #FFFFFF on the left and pale pink #F5BCE6 panel on the right covering 48.7% to 100% of the width. Clean flat minimal look. The left half is white #FFFFFF and the right panel is pale pink #F5BCE6. Titles and body text are black #000000. The accent is magenta #C31A8B on the pill, with the white label #FFFFFF. The typeface is a geometric grotesque sans-serif (close to OpenAI Sans / Söhne style). The title 'Ask for a summary' is regular weight at about 5.4% of slide height, 'Watch' is regular at about 4.4%, the subtitle and bullets are regular at about 3.2 to 3.5%, the 'Example asks' label is bold at about 3.2%, and the pill label is semibold at about 2.4%. Footer text is about 1.5%. Shapes have no outlines, no shadows, and no gradients. The pill has fully rounded ends (radius equal to half its height). Bullets are solid black dots with a hanging indent of about 2.2% of slide width.

### Slide 31

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Enlarge the body text and screenshots on the demo pages (slides 14, 15, 28, 29, 31) and fill or tighten the large empty areas on the text-light slides such as 8, 19 and 26, where the small text floats far from its title.

**Text**

Workflow 1 / Watch / Graph something useful / Turn the data into a visual / Examples: / Sales by week / Orders by category / Review scores over time / Most common complaint themes / OpenAI / SME AI Accelerator / 32

**Layout**

Two-column split: the left column (about 0 to 48% width) is white and holds a top row with a magenta pill label and an eye icon followed by the heading 'Watch', then the title, subtitle, and an 'Examples' bullet list left-aligned at about 2.7% margin. The right area (33.5 to 100% width) is a full-height gradient panel with a white screenshot card placed over it. The footer runs along the bottom left with the OpenAI wordmark and 'SME AI Accelerator', and a page number sits at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Chart: a line chart titled 'Weekly Transactions With Refunds Overlay' inside a screenshot of a chat interface. Y axis labelled 'Count by' with ticks 0 to 140 in steps of 20 (count, no unit printed). X axis labelled 'Week for' with ticks 0.00 to 10.00 in steps of 1.00 (week number). Legend at top of chart: 'Count by' and 'Week for' as dark grey chips with 'Y' and 'X' letters, then 'Transactions and' with a yellow-orange square and 'Refunds' with a red-orange square. Series 1, Transactions (yellow-orange line, about #FFB000): about 86 at 0.00, about 92 at 1.00, about 96 at 2.00, about 88 at 3.00, about 102 at 4.00, peak about 134 at 5.00, about 97 at 6.00, about 90 at 7.00, about 93 at 8.00, about 109 at 9.00, about 118 at 10.00. Series 2, Refunds (red-orange line, about #FF6A1A): about 3 at 0.00, about 4 at 1.00, about 5 at 2.00, about 4 at 3.00, about 6 at 4.00, about 10 at 5.00, about 8 at 6.00, about 7 at 7.00, about 6 at 8.00, about 9 at 9.00, about 11 at 10.00. No highlighted points. Gridlines are light grey dashed horizontal lines. Above the chart, the user prompt 'Create a simple chart of weekly transactions and overlay refunds on the same chart.' appears in a light grey bubble at top right of the card. Below the chart are the lines 'Here's your simple chart with transactions and refunds overlaid:', a pointing-hand emoji followed by an underlined 'Download the chart', a bold 'Quick insight:' heading, and a bullet 'Refunds are consistently low relative to transactions (good sign)', which is cropped at the card's bottom edge. Eye icon: a black outline eye with a circular pupil, inside a pale pink circle. Footer: OpenAI wordmark in bold black sans. The gradient panel shows soft orange at top left, pink and magenta at top right and right, lavender and violet at bottom left, blending to pink at bottom right. The screenshot card is cropped by the panel's bottom edge with no full frame.

**Design**

Background: Split background: white #FFFFFF on the left and a multicolour gradient (orange, pink, violet, lavender) #F9A07A to #FFB3F5 on the right 66.5%. Left half is flat white with black text. Magenta accent #C31A8B for the Workflow 1 pill and white label text. Title and body text are black #000000 in a regular-weight geometric grotesk sans-serif, with the title at about 5.4% of slide height and body and bullets at about 3.2%. The 'Workflow 1' label is semibold at about 2.4%. Eye icon circle is pale pink #F5B8E0. Right panel uses a soft multicolour gradient of orange #F9A07A, pink #FFB3F5, violet #C9B6FF and lavender #E3D2FF. The white screenshot card has a soft drop shadow, square-to-slightly-rounded corners of about 0.5% radius, and a light grey user bubble #F2F2F2 with rounded corners. Chart lines are 2px-equivalent smooth curves; axes are thin grey #9A9A9A text at about 1% size.

The footer (OpenAI wordmark and 'SME AI Accelerator') sits at the bottom-left at about 94.5% y, and the page number '36' sits bottom-right of the mint column at about 96.4% x.

**Visuals**

Eye icon: a simple black line-art eye (almond outline with a circular iris and a small dot in the centre), stroke about 2 px black, centred inside a pale mint circle badge next to the word 'Watch'. Circle badge is filled #B7F5E9 with no outline. Logo: the OpenAI wordmark in bold black sans-serif lowercase-style text at the bottom-left, a small monochrome black logotype, not coloured. No charts, photographs or tables. Bullets are solid black filled circles (●) used as the list markers in the Input and Output lists.

**Design**

Background is white #FFFFFF on the left and a flat pale mint #B7F5E9 panel on the right covering the right 51.3% of the slide with no gradient, shadow or border. Title text 'Spring flower sale campaign' is black #000000 in a geometric grotesque sans-serif (Graphik-like), regular weight, about 5.4% of slide height, set in two lines with tight leading. The pill label 'Workflow 2' is white #FFFFFF semi-bold sans-serif about 2.4% of slide height, on a deep teal-green pill #0A856B with fully rounded ends (radius equal to half its height). 'Watch' is black #000000 regular sans-serif about 4.4% of slide height. Body text (Goal line, Input/Output lists) is black #000000 regular sans-serif about 3.2% of slide height, with the labels 'Goal:', 'Input' and 'Output' in bold. Footer text is black #000000 about 1.5% of slide height. Accent colours are the teal #0A856B (pill) and mint #B7F5E9 (panel and icon badge). No rules, lines or dividers are visible.

**Text**

Workflow 2
Watch
Tool: Projects
Organize your campaign so everything stays
consistent, easy to find, and easy to reuse.
How to use it
1. Create a New Project
“Spring Promo”

location, etc.

OpenAI
SME AI Accelerator
37

**Layout**

Two-column split slide: the left column (about 0 to 49% of width) holds a pill tag and heading row at the top, title below it, then body text, a bold subheading and a numbered list, all left-aligned at x 2.7%. The right half (about 49 to 100% of width) is a full-height pastel gradient panel holding a centred dialog screenshot. The footer sits bottom-left with the OpenAI logo and 'SME AI Accelerator'; the page number '37' sits bottom-right of the left column area at x 96.5%.

**Visuals**

One illustration: a UI screenshot of a 'Project settings' dialog, a white rounded card with a soft shadow, shown straight-on and fully visible, placed on the right-hand gradient panel. Contents top to bottom: header 'Project settings' with an X close icon at top right; label 'Project name' above a text field containing a small pink gift/basket icon and 'Harbour Bloom Florist - Spring Promo'; label 'Instructions' with grey helper text 'Set context and customize how ChatGPT responds in this project.' above a multi-line text box containing 'Your job is to create assets to use during our spring promotion. Our business, Harbour Bloom Florist, is in Dublin near Grafton Street.' with 'Harbour' underlined by a red spell-check squiggle; label 'Memory' above a dropdown showing 'Default' with a chevron; grey note 'Project can access memories from outside chats, and vice versa. This cannot be changed.'; a red outlined pill button 'Delete project'; a divider line; bottom-right buttons 'Cancel' (white, outlined, pill) and 'Save' (solid black pill with white text). Also an eye icon in a mint circle beside the 'Watch' heading and an OpenAI wordmark logo in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Left half solid white #FFFFFF; right half a soft gradient from mint #8FE3CB at top-left through pale yellow-green #D9F7B0 at top to light green #A8EBA0 at bottom.

**Layout**

Two-column split: a white left column (about 0 to 49% of width) holds a header row at the top (green pill and icon with title, left-aligned at 2.7% margin) and the title 'Starter prompt' beneath it, with three row labels (Task, Context, Output) left-aligned at 2.7% margin and joined by horizontal leader lines to bracket marks at about x 54.5%. The right panel (about 49 to 100% of width) carries a soft green gradient with a white rounded prompt card at x 56.8%, y 29.8%, spanning to the right margin at 97.3% and bottom at 90.4%; footer 'OpenAI' and 'SME AI Accelerator' sit bottom-left at y 94.4% and the page number '38' sits bottom-right at x 96.4%.

**Visuals**

Non-text elements: (1) a green pill-shaped button behind 'Workflow 2' in white; (2) a pale mint circular icon badge containing a black line-art eye icon (open almond outline with a circular pupil) beside the word 'Watch'; (3) three bracket shapes drawn as thin black vertical lines with short horizontal serifs at top and bottom, at about x 54.5%, spanning y 32.2 to 38.0% (Task), y 40.8 to 52.0% (Context) and y 54.4 to 83.3% (Output); (4) three black horizontal leader lines, at about y 34.9%, y 46.0% and y 69.0%, running from about x 12.9% to the brackets; (5) the white prompt card with a soft grey drop shadow and rounded corners; (6) a black circular send button with a white upward arrow at about x 93.2%, y 82.7%, w 2.9%, h 5.1%, inside the card's lower-right corner; (7) a full-slide gradient image (1920 by 1080 px) of soft pastel mint, green, lime and light cyan blends, visible only in the right panel; (8) a placed 1000 by 855 px image at x 55.0%, y 26.9%, w 44.1%, h 67.0%, which sits behind the card and reads as its soft glow or shadow; (9) the small OpenAI wordmark in black at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Split background: solid white #FFFFFF on the left and a soft pastel green-aqua-lime gradient (#8FE0CC to #D9F7B8 to #A8E6A0) on the right. Left half is flat white #FFFFFF; right half is a blurred multi-colour gradient running from aqua #8FE0CC at top-left through pale lime #D9F7B8 at top-right to light green #A8E6A0 at bottom-left and #C5F5C0 at bottom-right. Title 'Starter prompt' and 'Watch' are black #000000 in a geometric grotesque sans-serif (OpenAI Sans-like), regular weight, title about 5.4% of slide height and 'Watch' about 4.4%. Body text is black #000000 regular sans-serif at about 3.15% of slide height; row labels Task, Context and Output are bold black #000000 at about 3.5%. Placeholder text in brackets is grey #8C8C8C regular. Accent pill is green #0A856B with white semi-bold 'Workflow 2' at about 2.4%. Icon badge is mint #B5F2E3. Leader lines and brackets are black #000000 about 1 px thin. Card is white with rounded corners of about 1% of width and a soft diffuse grey shadow. Footer text is about 1.5% black regular.

### Slide 38

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are many near-empty pages with small text and large dead space (slides 8, 19, 26, 28, 30, 38), several tiny and unreadable screenshots (slides 14, 15, 28, 36), and about a dozen gradient divider and name pages that add length without content.

**Text**

10 MINUTES / Try / Individual / Activity / Create your own / content kit / Create a Project, and make useful content: / Instagram / captions / Short email / One short / SMS / DM / Simple / in-store sign / Power-ups / Finished early? Try going up a level. / ● Document your brand voice with this prompt ↗ / ● Create images ↗ / ● Refresh your facts with Web Search ↗ / tinyurl.com/London-accelerator / OpenAI / SME AI Accelerator / 39

**Layout**

Two-column split: a white left column about 32.5% of the width holds the tag, title, QR code, URL pill and footer, and a full-height green right panel holds a heading, a row of four white cards and a mint callout box. Title sits top-left at about 15% from the top; the green panel content is left-aligned to x 35.1% with a right margin of about 2%. Footer 'OpenAI' wordmark and 'SME AI Accelerator' sit bottom-left at y 94.5%; slide number '39' sits bottom-right of the green panel at x 96.4%.

**Visuals**

Visual elements: (1) a black pill tag reading '10 MINUTES' in monospace; (2) a mint circular icon with a black spark/click-burst glyph beside the word 'Try'; (3) a square QR code (431 by 431 px) at about x 11.0%, y 67.7%, size 9.1% by 16.2%, black modules on white, with a white rounded centre tile holding a black arrow-in-circle icon; (4) a blue pill button with the URL tinyurl.com/London-accelerator in white; (5) an 'OpenAI' wordmark logo in bold black at bottom-left; (6) four white rounded cards in a row, each with a soft drop shadow beneath, and raster shadow images behind them; (7) a mint rounded callout box with a soft shadow containing three bulleted underlined links, each followed by a north-east diagonal arrow; (8) a soft raster shadow image under the mint callout box (1000 by 297 px). No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Split background: white #FFFFFF on the left and teal-green #0A856B panel on the right covering x 32.5% to 100%. Left half white #FFFFFF; right panel deep teal-green #0A856B. Title and subtitle text black #000000; the heading 'Create a Project, and make useful content:' and the URL text are white #FFFFFF; body text in cards and callout is black #000000; accents are mint #B7F5E9 and blue #3D8DFF. Typeface is a geometric grotesque sans-serif (close to OpenAI Sans / Söhne), regular weight for title and body, bold for 'Power-ups' and 'OpenAI'; the tag and 'SME AI Accelerator' use a monospace family for the tag. Title text about 8.9% of slide height (cap height about 6.2%); subtitle about 5.4%; heading about 4.3%; card and body text about 3.5% and 3.2%; footer about 1.5%. Cards: white, corner radius about 1.5% of width, soft grey drop shadow below. Callout: mint #B7F5E9, rounded corners about 2% radius, soft shadow. Links are black underlined text with bullet dots ●.

### Slide 39

**Text**

5 MINUTES
Share
Discuss at
your table
Show each other your best work
and borrow what’s working.
Share the one change that
improved quality the most.
OpenAI
SME AI Accelerator
40

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a white field: a top header row holds the black '5 MINUTES' pill, a mint icon circle and the word 'Share' at left. The large title sits top-left below the header, left-aligned at about 2.7% margin. The green checklist sits in the right half, starting at about 45% of slide width and vertically centred in the lower-middle area. The footer runs along the bottom with the OpenAI wordmark and 'SME AI Accelerator' at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Two line icons, both in the same style: a circled checkmark drawn in black, stroke about 0.2% of slide width, at the start of each green bullet line. A group-of-people icon (three simple head-and-shoulder outlines in black) sits inside a mint circle (#B8F5E8) to the left of 'Share'. A small OpenAI wordmark in bold black sits in the footer. No photographs, charts or diagrams.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background (#FFFFFF) with no gradient or texture. Title 'Discuss at your table' in black #000000, a regular-weight geometric grotesque sans-serif, about 8.9% of slide height per line with tight leading. Green bullet text in #0A856B, same grotesque sans-serif, regular weight, about 5.4% of slide height. The '5 MINUTES' label is in a monospace face, white on a black pill. 'Share' is black grotesque sans, about 4.4% of slide height. Accent colours are the mint circle #B8F5E8 and the green #0A856B. The black pill has full rounded ends (corner radius equal to half its height), no shadow. Bullet icons are black circled checkmarks with thin strokes.

**Text**

Build Recipe / Step 1 / Define the job / Who is it for? / When will they use it? / What job does it do? / What does success look like? / My assistant helps [who?] do [job?] by [process?]. / OpenAI / SME AI Accelerator / 46

**Layout**

Two-pill step tracker sits top-left with a short blue connector line between the pills, and the title Define the job sits left-aligned beneath it at the same left margin of about 2.7%. The body is a 2x2 grid of equal light-grey rounded cards in the middle (left column x 2.7 to 48.7, right column x 50.0 to 97.3), followed by one full-width white statement card at the bottom; the footer runs along the bottom with the OpenAI wordmark and SME AI Accelerator at left and the page number 46 at bottom right, all on a common 2.7% margin.

**Visuals**

Two rounded pill shapes form a step tracker. The left pill is a filled blue #3D8DFF capsule labelled Build Recipe in white. The right pill is a white capsule with a 2 px blue #3D8DFF outline labelled Step 1 in black. A thin blue #3D8DFF horizontal line joins the two pills. Four light grey #F5F5F5 rounded rectangles hold questions: Who is it for?, When will they use it?, What job does it do?, What does success look like? No photographs, charts or illustrations. Three soft blue #A3BEFA highlight rectangles sit behind the bold words who?, job? and process? in the bottom statement. A faint soft-shadow image (1000 by 125 px) sits under the bottom white card.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean flat white slide with no gradients. Title Define the job is a regular-weight geometric grotesque sans (close to Aeonik or Inter), black #000000, about 5.4% of slide height. Card questions are semibold/bold geometric sans, black #000000, about 3.5% of slide height. The bottom statement is regular-weight sans, black #000000, about 5.4% of slide height, with bold highlighted words. Step pill labels are bold sans, about 2.4% of slide height. Accent colour is blue #3D8DFF with highlight blue #A3BEFA. Grey cards #F5F5F5 have no outline and small corner radii of about 2% of slide height. The bottom white card has a subtle drop shadow. Footer text is regular sans, about 1.5% of slide height.

**Layout**

Single-column layout on a white field with a 2.7% left and right margin. A two-pill breadcrumb row sits at the top left (y 4.8 to 10.8), the title sits left-aligned below it at y 14.2, and three full-width stacked rounded cards occupy the lower half (y 35.8 to 90.4). The footer row at y 94.4 holds the OpenAI wordmark at left, the 'SME AI Accelerator' label beside it, and the slide number flush right at 96.4.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background (#FFFFFF) with black titles and body text (#000000) and a single blue accent (#3D8DFF) used for the filled 'Build Recipe' pill, the outlined 'Step 2' pill and the connector line between them. Three identical light grey cards (#F5F5F5) with generous internal padding and no border or shadow. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif similar to OpenAI Sans or Inter: regular weight for the title and card text, semibold for the pill labels and the footer, and bold for the lead-in phrases of each card ('What content/assets', 'What steps', 'What questions'). Title is about 5.4% of slide height; card text about 3.5%; pill labels and footer about 2.4% and 1.5%. Pills and cards use fully rounded ends and small corner radii (about 1.5% of slide width) respectively. No gradients or shadows.

### Slide 47

**Review notes**

- Review: The build recipe is repeated as a five-step tracker (slides 44 to 49), so the route is coherent and each activity page tells people what to do.

**Text**

Build Recipe
Step 3
What should the output look like?
Be explicit about structure, length, and tone.
Return a 6-line sign + a 120-word email + 1 SMS.
Return a table: Theme | Evidence | Fix | Owner | Effort.
Always include a quick ‘human check’ checklist.
OpenAI
SME AI Accelerator
48

**Layout**

Two-part header at top left: a pill-shaped step tracker (blue filled 'Build Recipe' pill, then a connecting line, then an outlined 'Step 3' pill) sits at y 4.8% to 10.8%, followed by a left-aligned two-line headline starting at x 2.7%. Three full-width white cards stack in a single column from x 2.7% to 97.4%, each about 16% high, at y 35.8%, 55.0% and 74.3%, with left-aligned text inset at x 5.2% and a round arrow button at the right edge of each card. The footer row at y 94.4% to 95.9% holds the OpenAI logo at left, the 'SME AI Accelerator' label at x 8.2%, and the page number '48' right-aligned at x 96.4% to 97.4%.

**Visuals**

Four elements have no photograph or chart. Three identical round black icon buttons, each about 2.9% wide and 5.1% high, sit at the right edge of each card (left edge about x 93.3%): the first at top about y 44.6%, the second at about y 63.9%, the third at about y 83.2%. Each button is a solid black #000000 circle with a white upward arrow glyph (a vertical stroke with a chevron head) centred inside. The three cards have soft grey drop shadows beneath them (light, diffuse, low opacity). The OpenAI wordmark in the footer is a small black logo at about x 2.7%, y 94.2%, w 5.2%, h 1.5%. The blue pill and connector line are described under elements.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, covering the whole slide. Clean, minimal light layout on a pure white background. Headline 'What should the output look like?' in black #000000 and the subhead in mid grey #8C8C8C, both in a geometric neo-grotesque sans-serif (Söhne/Inter-like), regular weight, with the title and subhead at about 5.4% of slide height (cap height about 3.8%). Card text is black #000000, regular weight, about 3.5% of slide height. The step tracker uses a blue accent #3D8DFF: the 'Build Recipe' pill is filled blue with white bold text, and the 'Step 3' pill is white with a 1 to 2 px blue #3D8DFF outline and black bold text, both about 2.4% of slide height. Cards are white with rounded corners (radius about 1% of slide width) and soft shadows. Footer text is about 1.5% of slide height in black.

**Layout**

A centred stack of three white lines sits in the upper-middle of the slide: the name on one line, then the role, then the company, with the name largest. A small footer row runs along the bottom edge, with the OpenAI wordmark and 'SME AI Accelerator' at left and the page number '53' at right. Margins are about 8% at left and right.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed background illustration: a soft, blurred abstract gradient of diagonal light bands. A bright pink-magenta band runs from the top edge near x 12-30% downward to the left-centre. A lavender-violet glow fills the top centre, and a pale blue to cyan area fills the top right. A saturated blue glow sits in the lower centre-right, and a pink-lilac band runs diagonally across the lower-left corner. The lighting is soft and diffuse, with no hard edges or visible objects. Composition is full-frame with no cropping, boxing or overlay. Dominant colours: pink #FF6FB5, lavender #B9A0FF, periwinkle #8FA8FF, sky blue #6FB4FF, cornflower #3D8BFF.

**Layout**

Two-column split. The left white column (about 0 to 32% of width) holds a black pill label, a circular icon and the title block stacked top-left, with empty white space below it and a footer row at the bottom left. The right column (about 33 to 100%) is a full-height gradient photograph carrying four stacked white rounded cards and a translucent panel, all left-aligned at x≈35%. Margins are about 3% left and 5% top; the footer sits at y≈94%, and the page number '61' sits bottom-right at x≈96.6%.

**Visuals**

A soft abstract gradient photograph fills the right column, full height, with no subject matter. It is cropped at the left edge of the slide's white area and runs full-bleed to the top, right and bottom edges. Colours: hot pink/magenta #E8579A at upper-left, periwinkle blue #6F9BF5 across the top centre, light sky blue #7FB8F7 at top right, lavender #C8A6F0 and pale pink #F2C4E8 in the middle, and lilac-blue #9FA8F5 at the bottom. The gradient is softly blurred with bokeh-like highlights. Overlaid on it are four solid white cards with soft drop shadows and a translucent white panel. No other illustrations or logos apart from the small OpenAI wordmark in the footer.

**Design**

Background is white (#FFFFFF) on the left and a multicolour gradient on the right. Titles and body text are black (#000000). The pill label is solid black (#000000) with white text (#FFFFFF). The icon circle is light blue (#BFD9FA). The typeface is a modern neo-grotesque sans-serif (close to Söhne), regular weight for the title and card text and bold for 'Power-ups'; the 'MINUTES' label uses a monospace face (closest common monospace, e.g. Roboto Mono or similar). Title 'Individual Activity' is about 8.9% of slide height (about 12 pt equivalent on a 5.6 in tall slide; capitals about 6.2% cap height), subtitle about 5.4%, card text about 3.5%, footer about 1.5%. Shapes are rounded rectangles with small corner radius (about 1% of slide height) on cards; the power-ups panel has a larger corner radius (about 2.5%). Cards have a subtle drop shadow beneath. The translucent panel is white at 50% opacity with a soft blur showing the gradient through.

Iterate, improve,

**Layout**

Centred composition on a full-bleed gradient. The title 'Make it reliable' is centred horizontally across the middle band (about 43% to 56% of the height). The two-line subtitle is right-aligned in the lower right, with its right edge near 97% of the width. The footer runs along the bottom margin, with the OpenAI wordmark and 'SME AI Accelerator' at left and the page number at right, about 94% down the slide.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed abstract image fills the slide: a soft-focus, blurred gradient of pastel streaks. A hot pink streak runs diagonally from the top edge at about 20% to 40% of the width down toward the centre. A lavender-to-violet haze fills the upper middle, a pale sky-blue band runs along the top right, and a saturated cobalt-blue glow sits in the lower centre-right around 60% of the width and 80% of the height. A thin pink diagonal streak crosses the lower left corner. Lighting is soft and diffuse with no hard shadows. Framing is full-bleed and uncropped, with no box, border or photographic subject. No logos or icons appear other than the OpenAI wordmark in the footer.

**Design**

Background is a pastel gradient image of blurred pink, violet, lavender and sky-blue streaks. All text is white (#FFFFFF) with no accent colours. The title and subtitle use a geometric grotesque sans-serif in regular weight, and the footer uses the same family, with the OpenAI wordmark in bold. The title is about 19% of slide height and the subtitle lines about 5.4%. The footer text is about 1.5%. No shapes, lines, corner radii, shadows or overlays are present. Two-zone layout: a full-height light blue panel fills the right 35% of the slide, and the white left zone holds a left-aligned text stack with its title at the top, a body block below it and a pill-shaped button beneath. A square QR code sits centred in the right zone and straddles the white-to-blue boundary at about 65% of the width. The footer runs along the bottom with the OpenAI wordmark and the programme name at left and the page number at bottom right, with a margin of about 3% on each side.

**Visuals**

One square QR code image, about 738 by 725 pixels, shown unboxed with no border or shadow and not cropped. It has black modules on white, three standard finder squares at top-left, top-right and bottom-left, and a central white rounded-square knockout holding a black stylised icon: a looped circular arrow that encloses a bold arrow pointing up and to the right. Lighting is flat and the image has no tint or overlay. It is pure black and white with no gradient. Also a blue pill-shaped button (#3D8DFF) with white text reading tinyurl.com/LondonSME, and the blue panel (#B7D4FE) behind the QR code's right half.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the whole slide, with a flat light blue #B7D4FE panel covering x 65 to 100 and full height. Clean, minimal, sans-serif style with a geometric grotesque look (close to Helvetica/Graphik-style neo-grotesque), regular weight for the headline and body, semibold for the button text. Background is white (#FFFFFF) with a flat light blue panel (#B7D4FE) on the right. Title text is blue #3D8DFF at about 8.9% of slide height. Body text is black #000000 at the same size, set with generous line spacing (about 9.6% line pitch). Accent colour is #3D8DFF used for the title and button. Footer text is about 1.5% of slide height, black. Shapes are flat with no shadows, no gradients and no outlines. The button is a fully rounded pill, about 50% corner radius.
